Master all BJJ transitions and techniques. Complete guide to sweeps, passes, takedowns, and escapes. Learn execution steps, and success rates.



### [[100% Sweep]]

Master the 100% Sweep from closed guard. Complete guide covering arm trap mechanics, hip angle creation, and sweep execution for competition.

### [[3-4 Mount to S Mount]]

Master the 3-4 Mount to S Mount transition in BJJ. Complete technical guide covering perpendicular positioning, arm isolation, and armbar setup mechanics.

### [[3-4 Mount to Technical Mount]]

Master the 3-4 Mount to Technical Mount transition in BJJ. Complete execution guide with leg posting mechanics, back exposure reads, and drill progressions.

### [[50-50 Entry]]

Master the 50-50 Entry from inverted guards. Step-by-step leg threading mechanics to secure 50-50 position. Success rates: Beginner 30%, Intermediate 50%, Advanced 65%.

### [[50-50 Entry from Standing]]

Master the 50-50 Entry from Standing in BJJ. Step-by-step execution guide covering leg entanglement mechanics, timing, and grip sequences from standing.

### [[50-50 Guard to Backside 50-50]]

Master the transition from 50-50 Guard to Backside 50-50 in BJJ. Learn rotational mechanics, inside control, and pressure shifting to gain dominant leg entanglement.

### [[50-50 Guard to Inside Ashi]]

Learn the 50-50 Guard to Inside Ashi transition. Extract from symmetrical entanglement to superior heel control. Success rates: Beginner 30%, Intermediate 50%, Advanced 65%.

### [[50-50 Guard to Outside Ashi]]

Master the 50-50 Guard to Outside Ashi transition. Clear opponent's legs to establish outside heel hook angle. Success rates: Beginner 30%, Intermediate 50%, Advanced 65%.

### [[50-50 Guard to Single Leg X-Guard]]

Master the 50-50 Guard to Single Leg X-Guard transition in BJJ. Convert symmetrical leg entanglement into an asymmetrical sweeping platform.

### [[50-50 Pass]]

Learn the 50-50 Pass in BJJ. Systematic leg extraction from dangerous entanglement to dominant position. Success rates: Beginner 35%, Intermediate 50%, Advanced 65%.

### [[Advance to Chill Dog]]

Master Advance to Chill Dog transition in BJJ. Progress from New York to gogoplata position with systematic leg threading and overhook control.

### [[Anaconda Choke]]

Master the Anaconda Choke in BJJ. Step-by-step execution from Front Headlock to submission finish with gator roll mechanics and arm-in choke details.

### [[Anaconda Control to Dead Orchard Control]]

Master the transition from Anaconda Control to Dead Orchard Control in BJJ. Grip adjustments, weight redistribution, and patient pressure mechanics for extended dominance.

### [[Anaconda Grip Break to Front Headlock]]

Master the anaconda grip break to front headlock in BJJ. Complete defensive guide for breaking anaconda control and transitioning to safer front headlock position.

### [[Anaconda Setup]]

Master the Anaconda Setup from front headlock. Learn arm threading, grip mechanics, and control principles for securing anaconda control position.

### [[Anaconda from Turtle]]

Learn Anaconda from Turtle in BJJ. Step-by-step execution from Turtle Top to Anaconda Control with rolling mechanics, grip details, and defensive strategies.

### [[Anaconda to Side Control]]

Master the Anaconda to Side Control transition in BJJ. Learn gator roll mechanics, grip maintenance, and finishing details to secure dominant side control.

### [[Angle Change Escape]]

Angle Change Escape from Gogoplata Control. Lateral movement defense to relieve throat pressure. Success rates: Beginner 30%, Intermediate 50%, Advanced 65%.

### [[Ankle Lock Escape to Standing]]

Master the ankle lock escape to standing in BJJ. Step-by-step guide to grip breaks, foot extraction, and safely returning to neutral standing position.

### [[Ankle Lock Finish]]

Master the Ankle Lock Finish from Ushiro Ashi-Garami. Step-by-step finishing mechanics, grip details, and transitions. Success rates by level included.

### [[Ankle Pick]]

Learn the Ankle Pick takedown in BJJ. Step-by-step execution from standing position to top control with setup, grip details, and defensive strategies.

### [[Ankle Pick Sweep]]

Master the Ankle Pick Sweep from seated guard. Step-by-step execution with grip control and off-balancing mechanics for sweeping standing opponents.

### [[Aoki Lock]]

Master the Aoki Lock in BJJ. Advanced shoulder lock from Aoki Lock Control using leg entanglement and hip-driven pressure. Setup, finish, and counters explained.

### [[Aoki Lock to Back Control]]

Learn Aoki Lock to Back Control in BJJ. Transition from shoulder lock control to dominant back position. Success rates: Beginner 30%, Intermediate 50%, Advanced 65%.

### [[Aoki Lock to Crucifix]]

Learn Aoki Lock to Crucifix transition in BJJ. Convert shoulder lock control to bilateral arm trap for dominant crucifix position with multiple submission options.

### [[Arm Drag]]

Master the Arm Drag in BJJ. Complete guide covering grip setup, angular back takes, and chain attacks with attacker and defender perspectives.

### [[Arm Drag Escape]]

Learn Arm Drag Escape from front headlock in BJJ. Use arm drag mechanics to clear head control and recover open guard. Complete attacker and defender guide.

### [[Arm Drag Sweep]]

Master the Arm Drag Sweep from closed guard in BJJ. Step-by-step execution, back take decision points, and defensive counters for this versatile sweep.

### [[Arm Drag from Clinch]]

Master the arm drag from clinch in BJJ. Complete guide to grip mechanics, timing, and standing back take entries with systematic execution breakdowns.

### [[Arm Drag from Clinch Bottom]]

Master the arm drag from clinch bottom in BJJ. Complete guide to grip mechanics, back take entries, and wrestling-based transitions for gi and no-gi.

### [[Arm Drag to Back]]

Master the Arm Drag to Back technique in BJJ. Complete guide covering setup, execution, and back take from butterfly guard with expert-level detail.

### [[Arm Extraction]]

Master Arm Extraction escape from Side Control. Learn systematic arm removal to recover guard. Success rates: Beginner 45%, Intermediate 60%, Advanced 75%.

### [[Arm Extraction and Pass]]

Learn Arm Extraction and Pass from Crackhead Control in BJJ. Systematic technique to clear trapped arm and transition to Side Control. Success rates: 30-65%.

### [[Arm Extraction from Clamp Guard]]

Master arm extraction from clamp guard in BJJ. Complete guide to freeing your trapped arm safely while avoiding submissions and maintaining top position.

### [[Arm Extraction to Turtle]]

Learn Arm Extraction to Turtle escape from Aoki Lock. Step-by-step technique to free trapped arm and recover turtle position for guard recovery.

### [[Arm Recovery from Crucifix]]

Master arm recovery from crucifix in BJJ. Systematic guide to freeing trapped arms through hip mechanics, angle changes, and guard recovery paths.

### [[Arm Recovery to Guard]]

Learn Arm Recovery to Guard from Kuzure Kesa-Gatame bottom. Step-by-step arm extraction to re-guard. Success rates: Beginner 30%, Intermediate 50%, Advanced 65%.

### [[Arm Triangle Setup]]

Learn the Arm Triangle Setup from North-South in BJJ. Step-by-step head-arm configuration, hip walk mechanics, and submission chain integration.

### [[Arm Triangle Transition]]

Learn the Arm Triangle Transition from bottom Hindulotine in BJJ. Reconfigure guillotine grip to head-and-arm choke. Beginner 30%, Intermediate 50%, Advanced 65%.

### [[Arm Triangle from Front Headlock]]

Learn Arm Triangle from Front Headlock in BJJ. Transition head-arm choke from front headlock control with systematic setup and finishing mechanics.

### [[Arm Triangle from Top]]

Master the Arm Triangle from Top in BJJ. Complete guide to setup from Side Control, walk-around finish, and chain attacks with defensive strategies.

### [[Arm in Guillotine Variation]]

Master the arm-in guillotine variation from top control in BJJ. Detailed guide to grip mechanics, trapped-arm finishing details, and escape strategies.

### [[Armbar Defense]]

Master Armbar Defense in BJJ. Learn systematic escapes including stacking, hitchhiker, and rolling defenses with position-specific strategies.

### [[Armbar Setup from Double Sleeve]]

Master the armbar setup from double sleeve guard in BJJ. Complete guide to sleeve control, hip rotation mechanics, and high-percentage submission entries.

### [[Armbar from Back Transition]]
